EDeuteronomy 32:1-43 derivation 2026-09-27 — THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 20, chapter 32, THE SONG, LEANThe book's twentieth reading, at the chapter's grain (chapter 32 verses 1-43 as one draft — give ear, O heavens — the witnesses called, the rain and the dew, the Name proclaimed, the Rock whose work is perfect; the crooked generation and the Father who made them; remember the days of old — the nations divided by the number of Israel, the LORD's portion His people; found in the desert, the apple of His eye, the eagle, the LORD alone; the heights of the land, honey from the rock, the blood of the grape; Jeshurun grew fat and kicked — the God who made him forsaken, demons and new gods; the LORD saw and spurned — I will hide My face, a foolish nation, the fire to Sheol; evils heaped — hunger, beasts, the serpent, the sword without and terror within; I would have blotted them out but for the enemy's boast — a nation void of counsel; had they been wise — one chasing a thousand, the Rock that sold them, the vine of Sodom; laid up in store — vengeance is Mine, the LORD will judge His people, where are their gods; see now that I, I am He — I kill and make alive, the hand lifted, the sword whetted, the blood avenged — sing, O nations; no portion edge inside the chapter — Haazinu is 32:1-52 whole, Vezot Habrachah opens at 33:1; the chapter the unit, CHAPTER NUMBERS; one chapter read at one sitting in four runs under the cost rules' cap by the split's byte plan, two units, one ledger), THE THIRTEENTH SITTING OF THE LEAN PASS (ruled 2026-09-23), its seventh reading and THE FIRST WITH THE SPINE IN FORCE since chapter 26: every row whole under the whole-row rule: Onkelos Deuteronomy 32:1-43 whole and fresh (the export's rows the DB's — the identity, asserted); THE SIFREI ON DEUTERONOMY IN FORCE — thirty-six piskaot 306-341 on 32:1-52 (249 rows read whole in both files, the export's longest piska 306 on 32:1 with thirty-seven rows; every head present, 328's misprinted in the Hebrew marker — 32:35 for 32:38's words, the row seated by its words; thirty rows read before over twenty-four ledgers REREAD WHOLE and marked by computation); eleven rows outside the spine citing the chapter read whole (eight reread whole from chapters 1-3, 4, 8, 9, 11, sitting 19 and the Genesis ledger of the covenant's bow; three fresh from the blessing's and the death's piskaot, the piskaot not opened — READ THEN COMPILE PER PORTION), the kin (Genesis 4, 6, 7, 11, 12, 15; Exodus 32; Leviticus 13, 26; Numbers 13, 14, 17, 19, 20, 23, 25, 27, 28, 35; Deuteronomy 4, 6, 8, 9, 11, 29-31) credited by name from the earlier ledgers. Ledger deu_32_haazinu_2026-09-27.md, coverage computed by script (312 sources over the chapter: 52 Onkelos + 249 spine + 11 outside), the ink facts computed from the Tanakh DB and the snapshot store (39 asserts: 1 fell on the first typed pass — 328's marker sits in the Hebrew row itself; 0 on the second and third), the engine's numeral parser measured on every verse (32:15's FALSE EIGHT "you grew fat" and 32:30's JOINED THOUSAND [1, 1002] — the spine reads the one as a verb and the other as four numbers: the compile's two guards), the store the DB at every verse but 32:13 (the qere), every quotation cut by consonants (zero misses on 312 rows in the whole-spine check's first run). Seated as claims DV32-01..12: give ear, o heavens, and i will speak; hear, o earth, the words of my mouth; my doctrine drops as the rain, my speech distils as the dew, as small rain on the tender grass and showers on the herb; i proclaim the name of the lord — ascribe greatness to our god; the rock, his work is perfect, all his ways are justice, a god of faithfulness without iniquity, just and right is he; they have dealt corruptly with him — not his children, their blemish, a perverse and crooked generation; do you thus requite the lord, o foolish people and unwise? is he not your father who bought you? he made you and established you; remember the days of old, consider the years of many generations; ask your father and he will tell you, your elders and they will say to you: when the most high gave the nations their inheritance, when he separated the sons of man, he set the borders of the peoples by the number of the children of israel; for the lord's portion is his people, jacob the lot of his inheritance; he found him in a desert land, in the waste, a howling wilderness; he compassed him about, he cared for him, he kept him as the apple of his eye; as an eagle stirs up her nest, hovers over her young, spreads her wings, takes them, bears them on her pinions — the lord alone led him, and there was no strange god with him; he made him ride on the high places of the earth, and he ate the fruitage of the field; he made him suck honey from the crag and oil from the flinty rock; curd of kine and milk of sheep, with fat of lambs and rams of bashan and he-goats, with the kidney-fat of wheat, and of the blood of the grape you drank foaming wine; but jeshurun grew fat and kicked — you grew fat, thick, gross — and forsook the god who made him and spurned the rock of his salvation; they moved him to jealousy with strange gods, provoked him with abominations; they sacrificed to demons, no-gods, gods they knew not, new gods that came up of late, which your fathers dreaded not; of the rock that begot you you were unmindful, and forgot the god who travailed with you; the lord saw and spurned, from the provoking of his sons and daughters; he said: i will hide my face from them, i will see what their end shall be, for they are a very perverse generation, children with no faithfulness; they moved me to jealousy with a no-god, provoked me with their vanities — i will move them to jealousy with a no-people, provoke them with a foolish nation; a fire is kindled in my nostril and burns to the depths of sheol, devours the earth and her increase, sets ablaze the foundations of the mountains; i will heap evils upon them, i will spend my arrows on them: wasted with hunger, devoured by the fiery bolt and bitter destruction, the teeth of beasts i will send on them with the venom of crawlers of the dust; without the sword bereaves, within terror — the young man and the maiden, the suckling with the man of gray hairs; i said: i would scatter them, i would make their memory cease from men — were it not that i feared the enemy's provocation, lest their adversaries misjudge, lest they say: our hand is high, and not the lord has done all this; for they are a nation void of counsel, and there is no understanding in them; had they been wise they would understand this, they would discern their latter end: how should one chase a thousand, and two put ten thousand to flight, except their rock had sold them and the lord had delivered them up? for their rock is not as our rock, and our enemies are judges; for their vine is of the vine of sodom and of the fields of gomorrah, their grapes are grapes of gall, clusters of bitterness; their wine is the venom of serpents and the cruel poison of asps; is not this laid up in store with me, sealed up in my treasuries? vengeance is mine and recompense, at the time their foot slips; for the day of their calamity is near and the things to come upon them make haste; see now that i, i am he, and there is no god with me; i kill and i make alive, i have wounded and i heal, and there is none that delivers out of my hand; for i lift up my hand to heaven and say: as i live for ever — if i whet my glittering sword and my hand take hold on judgment, i will render vengeance to my adversaries and recompense those who hate me; i will make my arrows drunk with blood and my sword shall devour flesh, with the blood of the slain and the captives, from the head of the leaders of the enemy. The Mishnah rows the spine names (Chagigah 1:8 at 317:3 and 335:1; Peah 1:1 inside 336:1-2; Sotah 9:9; Sanhedrin 3:5 the enemy's disqualification; Berakhot 1:1 the Shema's clock; Avot 5:1) are the compile's cases (20b); the full process (the docket whole, the full records) OWED to this chapter under the lean pass (World/step9/COMPILE_DEBT.md's lean-pass box).tested

The Sifrei (piskaot 324-328, twenty rows): THE CUP IN STORE — Psalm 75:9's drop drunk by the Flood, the Dispersion, Sodom, Pharaoh, Sisera, Sennacherib, Nebuchadnezzar, the golden cup mended and the earthen not (324:1), the deeds' fruit and both rewards deferred to the redemption (324:2-3); VENGEANCE IS MINE — Myself, not an angel nor a messenger (325:1), the fathers' deeds repaid (325:2), R. Yose's a fortiori on the calamity's nearness (325:3), haste for the nations and delay for Israel by the four kingdoms (325:4); THE LORD WILL JUDGE HIS PEOPLE — joy over the nations, regret over Israel by Genesis 6:7 (326:1 — the flood prologue's row reread), the seven signs of the hand gone: the captivity, the despair, the last coin, none to plead like Moses, Aaron, Phinehas, no helper (326:2-7); WHERE ARE THEIR GODS — of Israel and of the nations, the consuls and governors (327:1-2); the fat of their sacrifices — the army's provisions (328:1, THE MISPRINTED HEAD 32:35 for 32:38), THE WICKED TITUS in the Holy of Holies and the defective suffix (328:2), Moses' altar and the daily offering (328:3), THE NAME PROFANED PUNISHED AT ONCE (328:4). Onkelos: 'are not all their works revealed before Me, laid up in My treasuries', 'vengeance is Mine and I will repay at the time they go into exile', 'the LORD will judge the judgment of His people', 'when their strength is exhausted', 'where are their fears, the strong ones in whom they trusted, the fat of whose sacrifices they ate'. The ink: 'vengeance and recompense' and 'vengeance is Mine' once in the Bible; 32:36 QUOTED WHOLE in Psalm 135:14 (cited); vengeance thrice (32:35, 41, 43); 32:34 and 32:37 among the verses with no kin; the second person plural at 32:38. 		—Onkelos Deut 32:34, Onkelos Deut 32:35, Onkelos Deut 32:36, Onkelos Deut 32:37, Onkelos Deut 32:38, Sifrei Devarim 324:1, Sifrei Devarim 324:2, Sifrei Devarim 324:3, Sifrei Devarim 325:1, Sifrei Devarim 325:2, Sifrei Devarim 325:3, Sifrei Devarim 325:4, Sifrei Devarim 326:1, Sifrei Devarim 326:2, Sifrei Devarim 326:3, Sifrei Devarim 326:4, Sifrei Devarim 326:5, Sifrei Devarim 326:6, Sifrei Devarim 326:7, Sifrei Devarim 327:1, Sifrei Devarim 327:2, Sifrei Devarim 328:1, Sifrei Devarim 328:2, Sifrei Devarim 328:3, Sifrei Devarim 328:4witnessed
